@@ -0,0 +1,55 @@
+export const PDF_BITS = {
+  PRINT: 1 << 2,
+  MODIFY: 1 << 3,
+  COPY: 1 << 4,
+  ANNOTATE_FILL: 1 << 5,
+  FILL_FORMS: 1 << 8,
+  ACCESSIBILITY: 1 << 9,
+  ASSEMBLE: 1 << 10,
+  PRINT_HIGH: 1 << 11,
+export function permissionFlagsOf(word: number | null): PdfPermissionFlags {
+    return {
+      print: false,
+      modifyContents: false,
+      copy: false,
+      modifyAnnotations: false,
+      fillForms: false,
+      copyForAccessibility: false,
+      assemble: false,
+      printHighQuality: false,
+  return {
+    print: (word & PDF_BITS.PRINT) !== 0,
+    modifyContents: (word & PDF_BITS.MODIFY) !== 0,
+    copy: (word & PDF_BITS.COPY) !== 0,
+    modifyAnnotations: (word & PDF_BITS.ANNOTATE_FILL) !== 0,
+    fillForms: (word & PDF_BITS.FILL_FORMS) !== 0,
+    copyForAccessibility: (word & PDF_BITS.ACCESSIBILITY) !== 0,
+    assemble: (word & PDF_BITS.ASSEMBLE) !== 0,
+    printHighQuality: (word & PDF_BITS.PRINT_HIGH) !== 0,

+ * How a client reads a set a response carries: an enum, a literal or a
+ * union's kinds (docs/conventions/api-evolution.md, "Reading tolerantly").
+export type SetReading = { readonly closed: true } | { readonly unknownAs: string };
+ * Where a set keeps its reading: on its zod definition, so every copy of this
+function mark(schema: z.ZodTypeAny, reading: SetReading): void {
+export function readingOf(schema: z.ZodTypeAny): SetReading | undefined {
+  return (schema._def as MarkedDef)[READING];
+/** Marks a set a response carries as closed: adding a value to it is a breaking change. */
+export function closed<T extends z.ZodTypeAny>(set: T): T {
+  mark(set, { closed: true });
+  return set;
+ * An enum a response carries that can gain values: a value this client
+export function readsUnknownAs<T extends [string, ...string[]]>(
+  set: z.ZodEnum<T>,
+  unknownAs: T[number],
+  return reading;
+ * A union a response carries with a catch-all kind: an object whose
+ * `asCatchAll` adds what that kind has beyond the object's own fields. A kind
+ * the client knows is read as that kind, so a malformed one still fails: the
+ * catch-all is for kinds a newer server adds, not for broken data.
+export function withCatchAll<T>(
+  union: z.ZodType<T>,
+  unknownAs: string,
+  asCatchAll: (value: Record<string, unknown>) => Record<string, unknown> = (value) => value,
+    if (typeof value !== 'object' || value === null || Array.isArray(value)) return value;
+    const kind = (value as Record<string, unknown>)[discriminator];
+    if (typeof kind !== 'string' || optionsMap.has(kind)) return value;
+    return asCatchAll({ ...value, [discriminator]: unknownAs });
+  return reading;

diff --git a/packages/plugin/annotation/CHANGELOG.md b/packages/plugin/annotation/CHANGELOG.md
@@ -1,5 +1,33 @@
+- [#999](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/999) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – `chrome.marquee` is one look: a change replaces it whole, and a color it leaves out follows the accent again. The other `chrome` groups still merge per key.
+- [#1003](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/1003) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – The sub-capabilities are `AnnotationSelectionCapability`, `AnnotationDraftCapability`, `AnnotationTextCapability`, `AnnotationToolsCapability`, `AnnotationStampsCapability`, `AnnotationLinksCapability` and `CommentsCapability` (were `…Api`). `AnnotationToolDefinition` replaces `AnnotationToolDef`. Read shapes are `readonly`.
+- [#1000](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/1000) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – The selection is `selection.select(refs)` and `selection.getSelected()`, as page-edit, attachment and outline have them (was `set` and `list`). `CommentThreadChangedEvent` is tagged `type` (was `change`). The host contract's functions start with a verb: `getAuthority`, `getPictureEpoch`, `getPictureVersion`, `isPending`, `getDistanceCreationPage`. The snap settings are `alignmentThresholdPx` and `rotationThresholdDeg`.
+- [#982](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/982) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – A lent record with no live drawing keeps its picture while a new look is pending, instead of drawing its bare style in its place.
+- [#973](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/973) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – `selection.update({ subtype })` changes each selected annotation that can become that kind, as the same annotation, and the rest keep theirs. `selection.getProperties()` leads with a `subtype` choice when the selection's kinds are siblings that can change kind (not a replacement's strikeout), offering the kinds people may change by hand.
+- [#975](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/975) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – The caret works as in a word processor. With a caret in a text box, `selection.getProperties()` reports the text typed next: the style of the text before the caret (at the start of a line, the text after it). `text.toggleFormat()` and a font, size or color from `selection.update()` at a caret style the text typed next, without writing anything, and moving the caret drops that style. A selected box reads all of its text, so bold shows only when every word is bold, and a change styles all of it, a word with its own style included. `text.toggleFormat()` turns a format on unless all of the text already has it, so words that disagree all take it. A box restyled while it is already drawn live now draws in its new font at once, not in the font answer for its old style.
+- [#971](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/971) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – A shape or line tool's defaults may set `color: null`, so the tool draws without a stroke.
+- [#974](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/974) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – A free text box restyled after it was typed in is drawn in its new font at once: the fonts the engine answered for its old font no longer draw its body while its other runs follow the new one. An answer holds for the faces it was asked about; a box whose faces change is asked again, and typing keeps the answer.

diff --git a/packages/plugin/commands/CHANGELOG.md b/packages/plugin/commands/CHANGELOG.md
@@ -1,5 +1,21 @@
+- [#1006](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/1006) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – A command can have `labelParams(context)`: the values for its label's `{name}` slots, such as how many pages are selected. It's asked like `enabled`, again whenever the viewer's state or your app's changes. The values go into `label`, into the translated words under `labelKey` or the command's id, where a number `count` picks the plural form, and into every sentence that names the label. A `labelParams` that throws gives no values. Without the i18n plugin, `label`'s slots are filled too.
+- [#1003](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/1003) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – `CommandDefinition` replaces `CommandDef`.
+- [#1000](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/1000) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – A bound command's formatted shortcut is `shortcutLabel`; `shortcut` stays the key a command definition takes.
+- [#980](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/980) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – New standard commands: `tool:snapshot`, not allowed without `doc.content.copy` (`commands.notAllowed.doc-content-copy`); `snapshot:copy` (`Mod+C`), `snapshot:save` (a PNG named after the document and the page) and `snapshot:clear` (`Escape`), each enabled while there is a snapshot. `StandardCommandsPlatform` has `copyImage(image)`, and `saveFile` takes the file's content type. Commands that share a key take turns: a key runs the one that can run now, wherever it is in the list, so Escape lets a snapshot go even though cancelling a drawing has Escape too.

@@ -1,5 +1,23 @@
+- [#1004](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/1004) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – A fallback language in `loaders` now loads at the start, and a language shows once it and the fallback language are in, so a lazy fallback language no longer shows keys in place of its strings. Until then the fallback language shows, or, while that loads too, the first language in `locales`. A language in both `locales` and `loaders` shows its `locales` strings until its loader has run, and the loader's language then replaces them. When the fallback language can't load, `onLocaleLoadFailed` fires and languages show without it.
+- [#1006](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/1006) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – `interpolate(text, params)` fills a string's `{name}` slots as `t()` does, for text that has no translation. `TranslateOptions.params` takes a readonly record.
+- [#1000](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/1000) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – Follows core's settings events (`changedKeys`).

@@ -1,5 +1,21 @@
+- [#999](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/999) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – The `format` and `quality` settings read `null` while the engine chooses, so `updateSettings({ format: null })` gives the choice back; they used to read `undefined`, which a change could never set. `snapshot.box` is one look: a change replaces it whole, and a color it leaves out follows the accent again.
+- [#1003](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/1003) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – The full-page and tile settings types are `FullPageSettings` and `TilesSettings` (were `…Options`).
+- [#1000](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/1000) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – The tile settings name their units: `bleedPx` and `engageRatio` (were `bleed` and `engageAt`). Results say `mimeType`. The host contract's view demand ends with `destroy()`, and `getKeptAppearances()` starts with a verb.
+- [#980](https://github.com/embedpdf/embed-pdf-viewer/pull/980) by [@bobsingor](https://github.com/bobsingor) – `renderPage` renders part of a page: `rect`, in page space, cut at the page's edge (one that misses the page is `invalid-input`), sized by `width` or `scale`. `rotation` turns the picture. Either one renders for its caller alone, outside the layers' cache. New `snapshot` tool, added when the interaction plugin is there: drag a box on a page, or click for the whole page, and the box is held as the snapshot, `{ page, rect, image }`, its picture a PNG at `snapshot.scale` (default 2) pixels per point, turned as the page is shown, made smaller to fit 16 million pixels or the deployment's budget, and rendered once every change made before it is in. New `canSnapshot()` (`doc.render` and `doc.content.copy`), `getSnapshot()`, `clearSnapshot()`, `onSnapshotChanged`, the `snapshot` settings (`scale`, `box.fill`, `box.stroke`) and, on the host lens, `getSnapshotBox(page)`.

Before, it always asked for them first and, when refused, searched again without them: two requests for every search in a session without copy rights. `findAll(query, { snippets: true })` now refuses with `permission-denied` (naming `doc.text.copy`) without asking the engine. If the engine still refuses snippets the session looked allowed to have, the search falls back to matches without them as before.
